Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the biological structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 326. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 285 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_326(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 326. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^285(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The biological variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 744 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 968 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the biological space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 744 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 968. QED.
